{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"5-method lead-lag analysis: generalized CLI version.\\n\\nUsage:\\n    python run_5method_analysis.py --data_dir results/alpha_sweep/a000_s43 --alpha 0.0 --seed 43\\n    python run_5method_analysis.py --data_dir results/alpha_sweep/a000_s43 --alpha 0.0 --seed 43 --dry-run\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport glob as glob_module\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom statsmodels.tsa.stattools import adfuller\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse\\\")\\nfrom permutation_granger import permutation_granger_test\\n\\nN_PERM = 5000\\nALPHA = 0.05\\n\\nCANARIES = [\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"ece\\\"]\\nDOWNSTREAMS = [\\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\", \\\"perplexity\\\"]\\n\\nHIGHER_IS_WORSE = {\\\"ece\\\", \\\"perplexity\\\"}\\nLOWER_IS_WORSE = {\\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"distinct_1\\\", \\\"distinct_2\\\", \\\"distinct_3\\\"}\\n\\n\\ndef load_series(data_dir):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load metrics - from all_metrics.json if available, else aggregate gen_X/metrics.json.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    all_path = os.path.join(data_dir, \\\"all_metrics.json\\\")\\n    if os.path.exists(all_path):\\n        with open(all_path) as f:\\n            data = json.load(f)\\n    else:\\n        # Aggregate from per-generation metrics\\n        gen_dirs = sorted(glob_module.glob(os.path.join(data_dir, \\\"gen_*\\\")),\\n                         key=lambda x: int(os.path.basename(x).split(\\\"_\\\")[1]))\\n        data = []\\n        for gd in gen_dirs:\\n            mp = os.path.join(gd, \\\"metrics.json\\\")\\n            if os.path.exists(mp):\\n                with open(mp) as f:\\n                    data.append(json.load(f))\\n        if not data:\\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f\\\"No metrics found in {data_dir}\\\")\\n    data.sort(key=lambda x: x[\\\"generation\\\"])\\n    out = {}\\n    for k in CANARIES + DOWNSTREAMS:\\n        out[k] = np.array([d[k] for d in data])\\n    return out, len(data)\\n\\n\\ndef orient(series, name):\\n    return -series.copy() if name in LOWER_IS_WORSE else series.copy()\\n\\n\\ndef bh_fdr(p_values, alpha=0.05):\\n    pv = np.array(p_values, dtype=float)\\n    n = len(pv)\\n    valid = ~np.isnan(pv)\\n    adjusted = np.full(n, np.nan)\\n    if valid.sum() == 0:\\n        return adjusted.tolist(), [False] * n\\n    idx = np.where(valid)[0]\\n    p_valid = pv[idx]\\n    sorted_idx = np.argsort(p_valid)\\n    ranks = np.empty_like(sorted_idx)\\n    ranks[sorted_idx] = np.arange(1, len(p_valid) + 1)\\n    m = len(p_valid)\\n    adj = p_valid * m / ranks\\n    adj_sorted = adj[sorted_idx].copy()\\n    for i in range(m - 2, -1, -1):\\n        adj_sorted[i] = min(adj_sorted[i], adj_sorted[i + 1])\\n    adj_sorted = np.minimum(adj_sorted, 1.0)\\n    adj_final = np.empty(m)\\n    adj_final[sorted_idx] = adj_sorted\\n    for i, ix in enumerate(idx):\\n        adjusted[ix] = adj_final[i]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

from all_metrics.json if available, else aggregate gen_X/metrics.json.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    all_path = os.path.join(data_dir, \\\"all_metrics.json\\\")\\n    if os.path.exists(all_path):\\n        with open(all_path) as f:\\n            data = json.load(f)\\n    else:\\n        # Aggregate from per-generation metrics\\n        gen_dirs = sorted(glob_module.glob(os.path.join(data_dir, \\\"gen_*\\\")),\\n                         key=lambda x: int(os.path.basename(x).split(\\\"_\\\")[1]))\\n        data = []\\n        for gd in gen_dirs:\\n            mp = os.path.join(gd, \\\"metrics.json\\\")\\n            if os.path.exists(mp):\\n                with open(mp) as f:\\n                    data.append(json.load(f))\\n        if not data:\\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f\\\"No metrics found in {data_dir}\\\")\\n    data.sort(key=lambda x: x[\\\"generation\\\"])\\n    out = {}\\n    for k in CANARIES + DOWNSTREAMS:\\n        out[k] = np.array([d[k] for d in data])\\n    return out, len(data)\\n\\n\\ndef orient(series, name):\\n    return -series.copy() if name in LOWER_IS_WORSE else series.copy()\\n\\n\\ndef bh_fdr(p_values, alpha=0.05):\\n    pv = np.array(p_values, dtype=float)\\n    n = len(pv)\\n    valid = ~np.isnan(pv)\\n    adjusted = np.full(n, np.nan)\\n    if valid.sum() == 0:\\n        return adjusted.tolist(), [False] * n\\n    idx = np.where(valid)[0]\\n    p_valid = pv[idx]\\n    sorted_idx = np.argsort(p_valid)\\n    ranks = np.empty_like(sorted_idx)\\n    ranks[sorted_idx] = np.arange(1, len(p_valid) + 1)\\n    m = len(p_valid)\\n    adj = p_valid * m / ranks\\n    adj_sorted = adj[sorted_idx].copy()\\n    for i in range(m - lag]\\n        else:\\n            a, b = x[:n + lag], y[-lag:]\\n        if len(a) < 4:\\n            continue\\n        r, p = stats.pearsonr(a, b)\\n        all_lags.append({\\\"lag\\\": int(lag), \\\"r\\\": round(float(r), 4), \\\"p\\\": round(float(p), 6)})\\n        if abs(r) > abs(best_r):\\n            best_lag, best_r, best_p = lag, r, p\\n    return {\\n        \\\"method\\\": \\\"naive_xcorr\\\",\\n        \\\"peak_lag\\\": int(best_lag),\\n        \\\"r\\\": round(float(best_r), 4),\\n        \\\"p_value\\\": round(float(best_p), 6),\\n        \\\"significant\\\": bool(best_p < ALPHA),\\n        \\\"all_lags\\\": all_lags,\\n    }\\n\\n\\n# Method 2: Differenced cross-correlation\\ndef diff_xcorr(c, d, cn, dn, max_lag=5):\\n    dx, dy = np.diff(orient(c, cn)), np.diff(orient(d, dn))\\n    n = len(dx)\\n    best_lag, best_r, best_p = 0, 0.0, 1.0\\n    all_lags = []\\n    for lag in range(-max_lag, max_lag + 1):\\n        if lag >= 0:\\n            a, b = dx[lag:], dy[:n - {
  "server": "westd-19828",
  "command": "cd /root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \"\nimport json\n\nfor alpha_dir in ['a075_s43', 'a050_s43']:\n    path = f'results/alpha_sweep/{alpha_dir}/analysis/5method_leadlag.json'\n    data = json.load(open(path))\n    print(f'\\n=== {alpha_dir} ===')\n    \n    # Check pairs\n    pairs = data.get('pairs', [])\n    naive_fwd = 0\n    diff_fwd = 0\n    k3_count = 0\n    \n    for pr in pairs:\n        label = pr.get('pair', pr.get('label', ''))\n        fwd = pr.get('forward', {})\n        \n        # naive_xcorr\n        nx = fwd.get('naive_xcorr', {})\n        nx_lag = nx.get('peak_lag', 'N/A')\n        nx_sig = nx.get('significant', False)\n        \n        # diff_xcorr  \n        dx = fwd.get('diff_xcorr', {})\n        dx_lag = dx.get('peak_lag', 'N/A')\n        dx_sig_fdr = dx.get('significant_fdr', False)\n        \n        # perm_granger\n        pg = fwd.get('perm_granger', {})\n        pg_sig_fdr = pg.get('significant_fdr', False)\n        \n        # threshold_onset\n        to = fwd.get('threshold_onset', {})\n        to_sig = to.get('significant', False)\n        \n        # threshold_onset_3sigma (C008)\n        to_3s = fwd.get('threshold_onset_3sigma', fwd.get('threshold_3sigma', {}))\n        to_3s_sig = to_3s.get('significant', False) if isinstance(to_3s, dict) else False\n        \n        # ty_granger\n        ty = fwd.get('ty_granger', {})\n        ty_sig = ty.get('significant', False)\n        \n        # Count naive_fwd (M035: peak_lag < 0 + significant)\n        if nx_sig and nx_lag != 'N/A' and nx_lag < 0:\n            naive_fwd += 1\n        \n        # Count diff_fwd (FDR sig)\n        if dx_sig_fdr:\n            diff_fwd += 1\n        \n        # Consensus votes using 3sigma threshold (C008)\n        votes = 0\n        if nx_sig and nx_lag != 'N/A' and nx_lag < 0:\n            votes += 1\n        if dx_sig_fdr:\n            votes += 1\n        if to_3s_sig:\n            votes += 1\n        if pg_sig_fdr:\n            votes += 1\n        if ty_sig:\n            votes += 1\n        if votes >= 3:\n            k3_count += 1\n        \n        print(f'  {label:<30} naive(lag={nx_lag},sig={nx_sig}) diff_fdr={dx_sig_fdr} perm_fdr={pg_sig_fdr} thresh={to_sig} thresh_3s={to_3s_sig} ty={ty_sig}  votes_3s={votes}')\n    \n    # Summary\n    summary = data.get('summary', {})\n    print(f'\\n  PermG_fwd (FDR): {summary.get(\\\"perm_granger_fdr_forward_sig\\\", \\\"N/A\\\")}/8')\n    print(f'  naive_fwd (M035 peak_lag<0): {naive_fwd}/8')\n    print(f'  diff_fwd (FDR): {diff_fwd}/8')\n    print(f'  K>=3 (with 3sigma thresh): {k3_count}/8')\n    print(f'  K>=3 (summary robust_pairs): {summary.get(\\\"robust_pairs\\\", \\\"N/A\\\")}/8')\n\"\n",
  "account": "root"
}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I see a critical issue! The script's consensus has a BUG: it says "naive" is counted in the consensus for pairs where `peak_lag > 0`.

For a075_s43:
- token_entropy→distinct_1: naive_lag=5 (positive), nx_fwd=False. But script consensus says `[naive, diff]` → 2/5
- token_entropy→distinct_2: naive_lag=4 (positive), nx_fwd=False. But script consensus says `[naive, diff, perm]` → 3/5


- token_entropy→distinct_3: naive_lag=3 (positive), nx_fwd=False. But script consensus says `[naive, diff, perm]` → 3/5

The voting logic checks if naive_xcorr is significant AND peak_lag is negative before counting naive, but the JSON shows naive being counted for positive lags. This means the JSON was generated by an older version of the script that used the opposite condition (peak_lag > 0) before the M035 fix was applied.

Looking at the file timestamps and the backup file, the 5method_leadlag.json results were created before M035 was implemented, even though the current script on disk has the corrected logic. I need to regenerate these results with the fixed script. backup is `run_5method_analysis.py.bak_m035`. This could mean either:
1. The original file was backed up AS m035 (i.e., the backup IS the m035 version)
2. The original was backed up BEFORE m035 (i.e., the backup is the pre-m035 version)

The naming `.bak_m035` typically means "backup made during m035 change", so the backup is the PRE-M035 version and the current file is POST-M035.

But the JSON results still reflect the OLD behavior (naive counted with positive lags). This means the JSONs were generated BEFORE the M035 fix was applied to the script.

The dates: May 17 for the JSON files. When was M035 applied? I don't know exactly, but the fact that the current script has the fix and the results don't match means re-running is needed.

Now looking at a050_s43 more carefully — for token_entropy→distinct_1, the script shows [naive, diff, perm] with 3/5 consensus, but naive_lag=1 (positive), so M035 should exclude it, dropping to 2/5. Yet for ece→distinct_1, the consensus shows [diff, perm] only (2/5), and naive_lag=-1 (negative). This is backwards — the old script used `peak_lag > 0` which would count positive lags and exclude negative ones, so the consensus was definitely generated with that old filter.

I need to re-run both experiments with the current M035-corrected script to get accurate results.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The 5method_leadlag.json doesn't have a separate `threshold_onset_3sigma` field - it only has `threshold_onset` with a regular significant field. The C008 constraint says K value threshold voting must use registry `threshold_onset_3sigma` field. This means the 3sigma threshold check needs to come from a separate source (the registry or a recompute_3sigma script).

The current script's consensus uses `threshold_onset["significant"]` which is 0/8 for both experiments anyway. Since threshold_onset_3sigma isn't in the JSON, and the regular threshold is also 0/8, the K values computed by the script are already correct in terms of threshold contribution (0 for both threshold types).


Now I'm compiling the final results for the a075_s43 experiment - WikiText with alpha 0.75 across 11 generations. The config and 5method_leadlag files check out, with the latter re-run under M035 compatibility. Looking at the method performance: PermG_fwd achieved 5/8 on FDR, DiffXCorr_fwd got a perfect 8/8, and naive_fwd is showing partial results.

Continuing with a050_s43 - the alpha 0.50 variant with the same seed and generation count. This one also has the updated M035 files and shows PermG_fwd at 6/8 on FDR... So I'm confirming the FDR-corrected significance counts across different methods: naive forward has 4 out of 8 significant results based on peak lag timing, threshold onset shows no significant findings, and the consensus approach with K≥3 captures 3 out of 8. I need to check the registry for the 3-sigma threshold variant since it's not in the current JSON output. I need to check what's currently stored in the registry for these experiments to understand what needs to be updated.
```
